2026-10-21T15:08:12.5778984Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Raise concurrency to 208 on the 2-math-warpgroup + hot-4 replication configuration

Why: in run 39550627795 (2-warpgroup MegaMoE + hot-4 replication, c192)
the score stayed at 8366.32 (main 8356.18), even though the kernel bench
showed MegaMoE -11% against the old control. Prefill is no longer the
bottleneck. The prefill-0 queue was empty in 41.7% of batches (6.2% on
the 8258 run), and Decode ran 29.8 of 32 requests per rank. In a closed
loop at c192, throughput is about C / (TTFT + 200 x ITL) =
192 / (1.55 + 2.9) s. The Prefill headroom can only be used by declaring
a higher concurrency, which the task rules allow (team-declared N).

What, on top of fd122bc:
- configs/firmus-framework.json: concurrency 192 -> 208 (Prefill
  max_running_requests follows it). decode_extra_slots 16 -> 20, so that
  Decode slots (128 running + 20 x DP4 = 208) still cover every
  concurrent request, as test_firmus_reference requires.
- The launch test pins 208 on this branch. The server-info fixture
  changes are the same as main 719c719 / the extra-slots follow-up.
- Research note section for the c192 combined run.
Decode cap 128 (SwapAB limit), EAGLE3 3/4 and everything else are
unchanged.

Expected: Decode running rises toward 32 per rank and throughput by up to
128/119 = +7% if ITL holds (ITL SLA 20 ms, now 14.5 ms). TTFT rises with
the deeper queue but stays well under 3 s in steady state. More requests
in the start-up burst may miss TTFT, so the good fraction (98.5% now,
>= 90% required) is the main risk.
